Empirically, at the minimal hardware configuration (oscillator dimension $d_{\mathrm{osc}}$ = 2), oscillator attention outperforms softmax on keyword spotting (+1.00 pp) and on subject-verb agreement (+5.27 pp on hard sentences, with zero training failures versus one in five for softmax). On causal language modeling, where softmax retains an advantage, oscillator attention closes the gap as $d_{\mathrm{osc}}$ grows: from +11.09 PPL at $d_{\mathrm{osc}}$ = 2 to +2.98 PPL at $d_{\mathrm{osc}}$ = 32 on WikiText-2, and from +2.39 PPL at $d_{\mathrm{osc}}$ = 2 to +0.57 PPL at $d_{\mathrm{osc}}$ = 32 on TinyStories. The main objective of this work is not to replace softmax in software but to provide a mathematically grounded blueprint for accurate attention on physical substrates.

</details>


### [171] [Efficient Time Series Clustering from Multiscale Reservoir Dynamics with Granular-Ball Anchoring Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12077)
*Yifan Wang,Lifeng Shen,Shuyin Xia,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MSRGC-Net，一种高效的时间序列聚类框架，结合多尺度水库计算、颗粒球锚图构建和共识学习，在保持计算效率的同时实现了优于现有方法的聚类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列聚类面临聚类效果与计算效率之间的固有权衡：基于相似度的方法因成对距离计算导致二次复杂度，深度学习方法依赖昂贵的迭代训练和大量可训练参数。

Method: MSRGC-Net包含三个核心组件：1) 免训练的水库计算范式，无需反向传播即可提取多尺度时间表示；2) 颗粒球计算，通过密度一致性区域自适应建模数据分布，生成紧凑鲁棒的锚图表示；3) 基于共识的锚图优化策略，对齐多尺度水库表示并整合跨时间尺度的互补信息。

Result: 在广泛使用的单变量和多变量基准数据集上，MSRGC-Net在聚类性能上持续优于最先进方法，同时保持优越的计算效率。

Conclusion: MSRGC-Net通过无训练的水库计算和颗粒球锚图构建，有效解决了时间序列聚类中效果与效率的权衡问题。

Abstract: Time-series clustering remains challenging due to the inherent trade-off between clustering effectiveness and computational efficiency. Similarity-based methods often suffer from quadratic complexity caused by pairwise distance computations, while deep learning-based approaches typically rely on costly iterative training and a large number of trainable parameters. In this paper, we propose MSRGC-Net, an efficient time-series clustering framework that integrates multiscale reservoir computing, granular-ball-based anchoring graph construction, and consensus learning. MSRGC-Net adopts a training-free reservoir computing paradigm to extract multiscale temporal representations from raw time series without backpropagation, significantly reducing computational overhead. To capture the intrinsic structure of the resulting representations, granular-ball computing is employed to adaptively model data distributions via density-consistent regions, yielding compact and robust anchor graph representations. Furthermore, a consensus-based anchoring graph optimization strategy is introduced to effectively align multiscale reservoir representations and integrate complementary information across temporal scales. Extensive experiments on widely used univariate and multivariate benchmark datasets demonstrate that MSRGC-Net consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in clustering performance while maintaining superior computational efficiency.

</details>


### [172] [A Riemannian Approach to Low-Rank Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12120)
*Pratik Jawanpuria,Bamdev Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一黎曼几何框架用于低秩最优传输，将平衡与非平衡的低秩正定因子耦合建模为光滑子流形，并配备Fisher-Rao乘积度量，实现了高效的一阶和二阶优化求解器，在多种OT变体上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩OT方法依赖一阶镜像下降更新，需精细超参数调优且忽略优化曲率信息，缺乏对优化几何结构的建模。

Method: 将平衡/非平衡的秩r正定因子耦合建模为光滑嵌入子流形，配备Fisher-Rao乘积度量；推导黎曼投影算子、收缩映射和Hessian-向量积的闭式表达式；平衡情形使用共轭梯度和迭代Bregman更新，非平衡情形退化为闭式缩放。

Result: 每次迭代复杂度与数据集大小线性相关，提供秩充分性证书用于全局最优性验证，在多种问题规模上收敛速度和性能均超越现有SOTA低秩OT求解器。

Conclusion: 提出的黎曼几何框架统一了多种低秩OT变体，通过利用流形曲率信息实现了免超参数调优的高效求解。

Abstract: Low-rank optimal transport (OT) mitigates the quadratic scaling of classical solvers, yet existing approaches rely heavily on first-order mirror-descent updates that require careful hyperparameter tuning and ignore the optimization landscape's curvature. To address these limitations, we propose a unified Riemannian geometric framework for low-rank OT, modeling balanced and unbalanced rank-$r$ positive factored couplings as novel smooth embedded submanifolds of the positive orthant. By equipping these manifolds with the Fisher-Rao product metric, we derive tractable formulations for Riemannian projectors, retractions, and Hessian-vector products. Our cost-agnostic framework seamlessly extends to linear OT, Gromov-Wasserstein (GW), fused GW, and their unbalanced counterparts. For balanced OT, our geometric ingredients are computed via efficient conjugate-gradient and iterative Bregman updates. For the unbalanced OT, our operations elegantly reduce to closed-form scalings, completely eliminating inner iterative loops. In both regimes, per-iteration complexity scales linearly with dataset size, and we provide a rank-sufficiency certificate for global optimality verification. Extensive experiments across a range of problem sizes demonstrate that our regularization-free first- and second-order solvers achieve faster convergence and superior performance over existing state-of-the-art low-rank OT solvers.

</details>


### [173] [Unstable Features, Reproducible Subspaces: Understanding Seed Dependence in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12138)
*Gleb Gerasimov,Timofei Rusalev,Nikita Balagansky,Daniil Laptev,Vadim Kurochkin,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征的跨训练稳定性，发现稳定特征承载了主要的重构和预测信号，而不稳定特征虽个体不可复现但集中在可复现的低秩子空间中，反映了基模糊性而非纯噪声，并可通过池化跨种子特征构建更稳定的SAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE被广泛用于解释神经网络表示，但其学习到的特征是否在不同训练运行间可复现（即稳定）直接影响其实用性，目前缺乏对此问题的系统研究。

Method: 提出特征稳定性（feature stability）指标，估计每个SAE特征在独立训练中再次出现的概率；在多种种子、模型、层、字典大小和SAE变体下进行大规模实验；使用合成模型进行对照验证。

Result: 稳定特征承载主要的重构和预测信号；不稳定特征边际影响弱，被低频表层形式触发主导；不稳定特征个体不可复现但集中在可复现的低秩子空间；合成模型表明低秩真实特征可在子空间层面恢复但个体不可识别；池化跨种子特征可构建更稳定的SAE。

Conclusion: 不稳定特征并非单纯的失败或噪声潜变量，其个体功能影响弱但反映了可复现的低维结构，标准SAE对不同种子的差异源于基模糊性。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret neural network representations, but their utility depends on whether the learned features are reproducible across training runs. We study this question through \emph{feature stability}: for each SAE feature, we estimate the probability that a similar feature reappears in an independently trained SAE. This yields a scalable per-feature signal that separates stable from unstable features. In a large-scale study across seeds, models, layers, dictionary sizes, and SAE variants, we find a pronounced functional asymmetry: stable features carry most of the reconstruction- and prediction-relevant signal, while unstable features have weak marginal impact and are dominated by low-frequency surface-form triggers in both activation statistics and automatic explanations. Geometrically, unstable features are individually non-reproducible but concentrate in reproducible lower-rank subspaces, suggesting that seed dependence often reflects basis ambiguity within a shared region of activation space rather than pure noise. A controlled synthetic model makes this mechanism explicit, showing that low-rank ground-truth features can be recovered at the subspace level while remaining non-identifiable as individual SAE latents across seeds. Finally, by pooling unique cross-seed features, we construct more stable SAEs while preserving explained variance in this setting. Together, these results show that unstable features are not merely failed or noisy latents: they have weak individual functional impact, but reflect reproducible low-dimensional structure that standard SAEs resolve differently across seeds.

</details>


### [174] [PCA-Enhanced Adaptive NVAR Framework for High-Resolution Sea Surface Temperature Forecasting in the East Sea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12141)
*Sherkhon Azimov,Susana López-Moreno,Eric Dolores-Cuenca,JinYong Choi,Sangil Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合奇异值分解（SVD）与自适应下一代储层计算（Adaptive NVAR）的降阶预报框架，用于东海海表温度（SST）预测。SVD将高维SST场压缩为低维表示，Adaptive NVAR建模潜在状态的时间演化，最终重构为SST预报。实验表明该方法在多个预报时域上均优于标准NG-RC/NVAR，且SVD降低了计算复杂度，适用于实时海洋预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值海洋模型计算昂贵、不适用于实时预报；深度学习方法处理高维时空海洋数据存在困难，且在长时域预报中存在误差累积问题。需要一种高效、准确、可扩展的实时海洋预报方法。

Method: 采用SVD将高维SST场压缩为低维潜在状态，再使用Adaptive NVAR（自适应下一代储层计算）对这些潜在状态进行时间演化建模，最后将预测的潜在状态重构为SST预报。整体框架为降阶（reduced-order）架构，在降低计算复杂度的同时提升预报精度。

Result: Adaptive NVAR在多个预报时域上均取得比标准NG-RC/NVAR更低的预报误差；SVD有效降低了计算复杂度，使框架具备快速性和可扩展性，适合实时海洋预报。

Conclusion: SVD+Adaptive NVAR降阶预报框架在东海SST预测任务上优于标准方法，兼顾精度与效率，为实时海洋预报提供了一种有潜力的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of sea surface temperature (SST) in regional seas such as the East Sea is crucial for monitoring marine ecosystems, assessing climate risks, managing fisheries, and conducting naval operations. Traditional numerical ocean models provide reliable predictions but are computationally expensive and often unsuitable for real-time forecasting. Many deep learning methods also struggle with high-dimensional spatiotemporal ocean data and experience error accumulation over longer forecasting periods. This study builds on our previously proposed Adaptive Next-Generation Reservoir Computing (Adaptive NVAR) framework, initially introduced and tested on synthetic dynamical systems, and extends it to ocean forecasting. We present a reduced-order forecasting framework that combines Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) with Adaptive NVAR to predict SST dynamics in the East Sea. SST fields are compressed into a low-dimensional representation using SVD, which extracts dominant modes of ocean variability. Adaptive NVAR models the temporal evolution of these latent states, and the predicted states are reconstructed into SST forecasts. We evaluate the framework using regional ocean datasets and compare it with the standard NG-RC/NVAR. Results show that Adaptive NVAR consistently achieves lower forecasting errors across multiple prediction horizons. In addition, SVD reduces computational complexity, resulting in a fast and scalable framework suitable for real-time ocean forecasting.

</details>


### [175] [nD-RoPE: A Generalized RoPE for n-Dimensional Position Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12146)
*Boyang Li,Yulin Wu,Sizhe Xu,Nuoxian Huang,Zhonghang Yuan,Shangyi Guo,Shu Yang,Takahiro Yabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出nD-RoPE，一种无需分解即可推广到任意维度的旋转位置编码方法，通过连续希尔伯特空间中的平移不变性推导出各向同性的谱条件，并采用多尺度正则单纯形波矢设计，在图像、视频和点云任务中均取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RoPE扩展到高维领域缺乏统一的理论框架，大多数方法沿各轴独立旋转或经验性地混合频率，限制了跨维度交互并产生方向依赖的表示。

Method: 从连续希尔伯特空间中的平移不变性公式出发，推导出各向同性所需的谱条件（将位置和频率视为耦合的n维向量），并设计多尺度正则单纯形波矢实现非退化空间覆盖和对称的方向平衡二阶响应。

Result: 在图像、视频和点云上的实验表明，nD-RoPE在高维设置下取得了一致性的性能提升和更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: nD-RoPE通过理论驱动的设计，在不依赖维度分解的前提下实现了各向同性的高维位置编码，为Transformer模型在高维数据中的应用提供了统一且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) is widely adopted in Transformer models, yet its extension to high-dimensional domains lacks a unified theoretical formulation. Most existing approaches either apply rotations independently along each axis or empirically mix frequencies, which limits cross-dimensional interactions and yields direction-dependent representations. To address these limitations, we propose nD-RoPE, a decomposition-free generalization of RoPE to arbitrary dimensions. From a translation-invariant formulation in continuous Hilbert space, we derive a spectral condition for isotropy that requires treating positions and frequencies as coupled \(n\)-dimensional vectors. We instantiate this formulation with a multi-scale regular-simplex wave-vector design, which provides non-degenerate spatial coverage and a symmetric, directionally balanced second-order response. Experiments across images, videos, and point clouds demonstrate consistent performance gains and improved generalization in high-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [176] [How Low Can You Go? Active Learning for Sparse Model Discovery in the Ultra-Low-Data Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12182)
*Ana Larrañaga,Urban Fasel,Steven L. Brunton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于主动学习策略（结合E-SINDy）的超低数据量下动力学系统方程发现方法，通过迭代优先采样最富信息区域，在Lorenz、Burgers和Kuramoto-Sivashinsky等系统中以远少于随机采样的数据量准确识别控制方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中数据采集成本高昂，现有数据驱动方法在超低数据量下难以有效发现动力学系统的控制方程，亟需一种主动采样策略来最大化有限数据的利用效率。

Method: 基于SINDy框架及其集成扩展E-SINDy来估计认知不确定性，通过迭代优先采样不确定性最高的区域（即对模型识别最富信息的区域），分别处理常微分方程（如Lorenz系统）和偏微分方程（如Burgers方程和Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程）。

Result: 在所有测试系统（Lorenz、Burgers、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky）中，主动学习策略均能以显著少于随机采样的数据样本准确识别出控制方程，且在不同数据预算和噪声水平下均保持有效性。

Conclusion: 主动学习策略可大幅降低动力学发现所需的数据量，为高成本数据采集场景下的控制方程辨识提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Identifying the governing equations of complex dynamical systems remains a fundamental challenge across science and engineering. While early approaches relied on empirical data and heuristics, modern data-driven methods offer greater flexibility and fewer assumptions. However, data acquisition in real-world settings is often expensive. This work addresses this challenge by introducing an active learning strategy for dynamics discovery in the ultra-low data limit. Rather than sampling randomly, our method iteratively prioritizes regions that are most informative for model identification. This approach builds on Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy), and utilizes an ensemble extension, E-SINDy, to estimate epistemic uncertainty and guide the sampling for both ordinary and partial differential equations (ODEs/PDEs). For ODEs, an exhaustive analysis is conducted on the Lorenz system across varying data budgets and noise levels. For PDEs, two systems with contrasting dynamical characteristics are examined: the Burgers' equation, where a sharp shock front creates a distinction between informative and uninformative regions, and the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation, which presents a more spatially complex sampling landscape. Across all scenarios, the proposed method accurately identifies the governing dynamics with significantly fewer data samples than random sampling.

</details>


### [177] [Implicit Neural Representations of Individual Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12200)
*Andrew Kang,Priya Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Behavioral INR，一种自监督生成模型，将隐式神经表示（INR）从视觉领域迁移到行为策略表示学习。该模型将策略表示为状态-动作函数，通过FiLM层调节的片段级隐变量实现无监督策略识别，并自然适应变长片段和不同采样粒度。在多个基准上，Behavioral INR在连续状态-动作空间中显著提升策略可识别性，尤其在长片段、多策略和分布外偏移场景下优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为数据集（如机器人示范、游戏回放、竞速数据）常包含多种未标注策略的混合数据，缺乏有效的无监督策略表示学习方法；同时，标准行为分布外检测仅关注新智能体或环境，忽略了策略间状态分布和动作分布偏移的情况。

Method: 将视觉领域的隐式神经表示（INR）适配到行为策略：策略被建模为从状态到动作的连续函数，片段级潜变量通过FiLM层调制该函数，形成策略的生成先验；变长片段和不同采样粒度自然兼容，类似INR处理不同分辨率图像。

Result: 在合成高斯随机场、MuJoCo示范、国际象棋、F1赛车、机器人操作和Seek-Avoid等数据集上，Behavioral INR在连续状态-动作的最困难设置中持续提升策略可识别性，尤其在长片段、多策略和OOD偏移场景下；而基于历史编码的基线方法仅在符号性重复或低维动作统计可区分时具有竞争力。

Conclusion: Behavioral INR为无标签多策略行为数据提供了一种有效的表示学习框架，在复杂连续控制任务中优于现有方法，但对高度符号化或低维统计模式依赖的场景优势有限。

Abstract: We study policy representation learning from unlabeled multi-policy behavioral data. Each episode is generated by a fixed policy, but policy labels are unavailable. This setting appears in robotics play, demonstrations, games, racing, and other datasets where heterogeneous behaviors are mixed without annotations. We introduce \emph{Behavioral INR}, a self-supervised generative model that adapts implicit neural representations (INRs) from vision to behavior. Instead of mapping coordinates to RGB values, Behavioral INR represents a policy as a state-action function mapping states to subsequent actions. An episode-level latent modulates this function through FiLM layers, yielding a generative prior over policies and allowing policy identity to be inferred without supervision. Because INRs treat each datapoint as samples from an underlying function, the same model naturally accommodates variable episode lengths and different sampling granularities, as in vision INRs with different image resolutions. We also define policy-level out-of-distribution (OOD) shifts along state-distribution and action-distribution axes, which arise when policies overlap in states or actions but are not captured by standard behavioral OOD settings based only on new agents or environments. We evaluate on synthetic Gaussian random field data, MuJoCo demonstrations with controlled OOD splits, and real-world chess, Formula 1 racing, robotics, and Seek-Avoid datasets. Behavioral INR most consistently improves policy identifiability in the hardest continuous state-action settings, especially when longer episodes, more policies, and OOD splits reduce the usefulness of marginal shortcuts; amortized history encoders remain competitive when policy identity can be recovered from symbolic repetition or low-dimensional action statistics. We release code and checkpoints.

</details>


### [178] [Re-evaluating Confidence Remasking in Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12232)
*Stipe Frkovic,Metod Jazbec,Dan Zhang,Christian A. Naesseth,Ilija Bogunovic,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了掩码扩散语言模型中基于置信度的后处理重掩码方法（以WINO为代表），发现在标准解码设置（短块长度）下几乎没有带来增益，在非贪心解码中虽能部分缓解随机性引入的错误，但会加剧多样性崩溃问题，表明该类方法的收益高度依赖于具体设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（dLLMs）因支持并行生成而成为自回归模型的替代方案，但其"一旦解掩码便不可修改"的机制导致早期采样错误无法修正。为此，研究者提出了无需训练的、基于置信度的后处理重掩码方法（如WINO），本文旨在对该类方法进行更全面的实证评估。

Method: 本文以WINO为代表的后处理重掩码方法为研究对象，在标准解码设置（短块长度）和非贪心解码设置下，与仅基于置信度的解掩码方法（Wu et al., 2025）进行对比实验，评估生成质量和多样性。

Result: 1）在标准解码设置（短块长度）下，基于置信度的重掩码方法相比仅使用置信度解掩码几乎没有带来改进；2）在非贪心解码中，该方法能部分缓解随机性增大带来的错误，但会加剧置信度解掩码已有的多样性崩溃问题。

Conclusion: 基于置信度的后处理重掩码方法的收益高度依赖于解码设置，并非普遍有效，这凸显了建立更全面的评估框架的必要性。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive language models, with the promise of faster inference via parallel token generation. A notable limitation of the masked formulation, however, is that once a token has been unmasked it can no longer be revised, leaving dLLMs vulnerable to early sampling mistakes. To address this, a growing body of work has sought to extend masked dLLMs with self-correcting (remasking) capabilities. One appealing subset of these methods does so in a training-free, post-hoc manner based on token confidences, with encouraging early reported results. In this work, we revisit the empirical evaluation of a representative post-hoc remasking method, WINO [Hong et al., 2026], and find that under standard decoding settings (shorter block lengths) it brings little-to-no benefit over confidence-based unmasking alone [Wu et al., 2025]. Extending the evaluation to non-greedy decoding, we find that while confidence-based remasking can mitigate errors introduced by increased stochasticity to some extent, it also exacerbates the diversity collapse previously reported for confidence-based unmasking. Overall, our results show that the benefits of post-hoc confidence-based remasking are highly setting-dependent, underscoring the need for a more comprehensive evaluation framework.

</details>


### [179] [Multi-Rate Mixture of Experts for Accelerating Liquid Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12240)
*Shilong Zong,Almuatazbellah Boker,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于液态神经网络的多速率混合专家框架（MR-MoE），通过多尺度专家分解、自适应门控网络以及特征级与时间级注意力机制，有效建模多变量时间序列中的复杂时序依赖、不规则采样和异质动态，在预测任务中优于LSTM、单LNN和标准MoE基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RNN（如LSTM）在离散时间步上运行，难以捕捉连续且不规则的时序行为；标准液态神经网络虽具备连续时间动态，但依赖单一动力系统，无法充分建模多时间尺度上的异质时序模式。

Method: 构建多速率混合专家框架：1）多个LNN专家分别运行在不同时间尺度上，分离快变与慢变动态；2）门控网络根据输入条件实现自适应专家分配；3）特征级注意力抑制噪声变量，时间级注意力聚焦历史关键状态。

Result: 在复杂多变量时间序列预测任务中，MR-MoE在AUROC和AUPRC指标上持续优于LSTM、单LNN和标准MoE基线，同时保持良好计算效率。

Conclusion: 结合连续时间动态、多尺度专家分解与自适应注意力机制，能有效提升时间序列建模性能，证明了该框架在处理复杂时序数据上的优势。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series data often exhibit complex temporal dependencies, irregular sampling, and heterogeneous dynamics across multiple time scales, making accurate sequence modeling particularly challenging. Traditional recurrent neural networks (RNNs), such as Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, operate in discrete time and may struggle to effectively capture continuous and irregular temporal behaviors. Liquid Neural Networks (LNNs) address some of these limitations through continuous-time dynamics, but standard LNN architectures typically rely on a single dynamical system, limiting their ability to model heterogeneous temporal patterns. To address these challenges, we propose a Multi-Rate Mixture-of-Experts (MR-MoE) framework built on top of Liquid Neural Networks. In the proposed architecture, multiple LNN-based experts operate at distinct time scales, enabling the model to explicitly separate fast-changing dynamics from slow-evolving temporal trends. A gating network further enables adaptive expert specialization based on input conditions. In addition, we incorporate both feature-level and temporal attention mechanisms to improve robustness, interpretability, and long-range dependency modeling. Feature-level attention suppresses noisy or irrelevant variables, while temporal attention selectively focuses on informative historical states. We evaluate the proposed framework on a complex multivariate time-series prediction task and compare it against strong baselines, including LSTM, monolithic LNN, and standard MoE models. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed MR-MoE framework consistently achieves improved AUROC and AUPRC performance while maintaining favorable computational efficiency. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining continuous-time dynamics, multi-scale expert decomposition, and adaptive attention mechanisms for time-series modeling.

</details>


### [180] [Reinforcement Learning Disrupts Gradient-Based Adversarial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12251)
*Xinhai Zou,Chang Zhao,Alireza Aghabagherloo,Dave Singelée,Robin Degraeve,Bart Preneel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用强化学习（RL）训练图像分类器，通过策略梯度目标和ε-贪婪探索破坏梯度结构，从而有效抵御基于梯度的对抗攻击。实验表明RL训练使梯度方向高度不稳定且幅度减小，导致PGD等攻击在有限迭代内失效；进一步结合对抗训练（RL-adv）形成梯度级+边界级双层防御，在多种攻击上超越传统监督学习对抗训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的对抗攻击是DNN的主要威胁，现有防御方法主要关注边界鲁棒性（如对抗训练），但鲜有研究从梯度信息本身入手。作者试图探索RL训练能否通过破坏攻击者依赖的梯度结构，提供一种互补的防御机制。

Method: 使用策略梯度目标和ε-贪婪探索训练图像分类器（而非标准监督学习），在CIFAR-10/100和ImageNet-100上通过多个架构进行系统实验。通过损失景观可视化、静态/动态梯度指标和预测熵进行机制分析。进一步将RL与对抗训练结合（RL-adv）形成双层防御。

Result: RL训练的分类器显著破坏了基于梯度的对抗优化；RL作为隐式正则化器产生高度不稳定的梯度方向和较小的梯度幅度，使PGD每一步都方向不可靠、幅度受限；RL-adv在PGD、AutoAttack、迁移攻击和查询攻击上均取得最高鲁棒性，显著优于SL-adv。

Conclusion: RL诱导的梯度破坏是一种互补的鲁棒性机制，与对抗训练协同作用；未来可研究混合SL-RL训练策略，结合SL的高效性和RL的梯度正则化特性。

Abstract: Gradient-based adversarial attacks remain a dominant threat to deep neural networks (DNNs), as they exploit gradient information to efficiently optimize adversarial perturbations. To address this, we investigate whether reinforcement learning (RL) training can disrupt the gradient structure used by attackers by training image classifiers with policy-gradient objectives and epsilon-greedy exploration. Through systematic experiments across CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-100 with multiple architectures, we find that RL-trained classifiers significantly disrupt gradient-based adversarial optimization. To explain this, we conduct a comprehensive mechanism analysis using loss landscape visualization, static and dynamic gradient indicators, and predictive entropy. Our analysis reveals that RL acts as an implicit regularizer, producing models with highly unstable gradient directions and smaller gradient magnitudes. This combination makes each PGD step both unreliable in direction and limited in magnitude, causing gradient-based attacks to fail within practical iteration budgets. We further show that combining RL with adversarial training (RL-adv) provides a dual-layer defense operating at two complementary levels: RL degrades gradient information available to attackers (gradient-level defense), while adversarial training strengthens decision boundaries (boundary-level defense). RL-adv achieves the highest robustness across all major attack types evaluated, including gradient-based (PGD, AutoAttack), transfer-based, and query-based attacks, outperforming SL-adv by a significant margin. These findings identify RL-induced gradient disruption as a complementary robustness mechanism and motivate future research on hybrid SL-RL training schedules that combine SL's efficiency with RL's gradient-regularization properties.

</details>


### [181] [Using Explainability as a Training-Time Reliability Signal for Efficient ECG Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12252)
*Veerendhra Kumar Dangeti,Xiao Gu,Ying Weng,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ERTS通过利用解释质量（Grad-CAM注意力图）作为训练信号，在ECG分类中过滤低质量样本，从而在降低训练成本的同时提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列分析（尤其是心电图分类）中，深度神经网络训练成本高昂，而现有方法如Progressive Data Dropout依赖模型置信度筛选样本，可能保留噪声或模糊样本而非有信息量的样本，导致效率与可靠性不足。

Method: 提出ERTS框架：在训练过程中计算候选样本的Grad-CAM注意力图，导出焦点分数（focus score），衡量模型预测是否基于连贯且局部化的模式。低焦点样本被过滤，高焦点样本优先用于梯度更新，实现渐进式数据选择。

Result: 在三个ECG数据集和多种骨干架构上，ERTS在降低有效训练成本的同时，持续提升了macro-F1指标。

Conclusion: 解释质量可以作为有效的训练信号，在临床时间序列学习中同时提升训练效率和模型可靠性。

Abstract: Training deep neural networks for clinical time-series analysis is computationally demanding, yet many healthcare settings lack the resources required for repeated model development and deployment. This challenge is particularly evident in electrocardiogram classification, where large datasets and long training schedules make efficiency practically important. Progressive Data Dropout reduces training cost by excluding samples from gradient updates once they are learned, but it relies on model confidence and may retain samples that are difficult due to noise or ambiguity rather than useful signal. In this work, we introduce ERTS, an explainability-based reliability training signal for efficient ECG classification. ERTS uses explanation quality during training to distinguish between informative and unreliable uncertainty. Building on progressive data selection, we compute Grad-CAM attention maps for candidate samples and derive a focus score that measures whether model predictions are supported by coherent and localised patterns. Samples with low focus are filtered out, while those with meaningful attention are prioritised for gradient updates. We evaluate ERTS across three ECG datasets and multiple backbone architectures, showing consistent improvements in macro-F1 alongside reduced effective training cost. These results suggest that explanation quality can serve as a practical signal for improving both efficiency and reliability in clinical time-series learning. Code will be released.

</details>


### [182] [Finding Multiple Interpretations in Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12277)
*Matthew Chak,Paul Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方法，用于寻找在损失/准确率等性能指标上相似但具有高度不同上下文感知特征（如基因表达模式）的模型集合。通过在METABRIC数据集上的实验，该方法在不牺牲性能的前提下发现了与对照方法截然不同的基因表达模型。作者认为，该方法对于分析模型的全局特征以洞察潜在现象具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法倾向于找到性能相似但特征同质化的模型，难以揭示不同机制如何实现同等性能。作者希望找到性能相似但特征（如基因表达）高度不同的模型，从而更全面地理解底层现象。

Method: 提出一种基于上下文感知特征的搜索方法，用于发现性能相似但特征差异显著的模型集合。在METABRIC乳腺癌数据集上，通过比较模型在损失/准确率上的表现以及基因表达模式的差异来验证方法有效性。

Result: 在METABRIC数据集上，该方法成功找到了多个与对照方法相比具有高度不同基因表达模式的模型，且未导致性能损失。

Conclusion: 该方法为分析模型全局特征以提取对潜在现象的洞察提供了重要工具，尤其适用于需要从模型特征中理解数据底层机制的研究场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an approach to finding sets of similar-performing models (in terms of loss/accuracy measurements) with highly different context-aware characteristics. Through experiments on the METABRIC dataset, we show that the proposed method finds multiple models with highly different gene expressions than those found by the control methodology without performance penalties. We argue that the proposed methodology is important whenever one aims to analyze any global characteristic of a model to extract insight into the underlying phenomenon being studied.

</details>


### [183] [Holding the FP8 Quality Ceiling at 8-Bit Weights and Activations: INT8 and GGUF Post-Training Quantization of Ideogram 4.0 for Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12280)
*Deep Gandhi,Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Ideogram 4.0（93亿参数的流匹配扩散Transformer）在缺乏FP8张量核心的Ampere RTX 3090 GPU上进行了INT8 W8A8后训练量化。结果表明，INT8在质量上与FP8持平（引导自举CI包含零），优于NF4（CLIP提升+1.9），且GGUF Q4_K在质量-内存前沿上为Pareto最优。研究还揭示了FFN下投影保护是关键质量杠杆，并指出INT8在Ampere上的速度提升需依赖融合INT8内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大文本到图像扩散Transformer的后训练量化虽能在消费级GPU上运行，但硬件特定的质量-效率权衡鲜有直接测量。本文旨在填补这一空白，针对缺乏FP8张量核心的Ampere架构GPU，系统评估INT8量化的可行性。

Method: 对Ideogram 4.0（9.3B流匹配DiT，双权重副本用于无分类器引导，Qwen3-VL-8B编码器）应用INT8 W8A8量化方案：每通道权重、每token动态激活、SmoothQuant，以及对高脆弱层集合的混合精度保护。在200提示基准上使用配对同种子bootstrap置信区间进行质量评估，并进行逐类别OCR分析和消融实验。

Result: INT8与FP8质量持平（CI包含零），INT8比NF4提升+1.9 CLIP（95% CI [1.21, 2.64]）。GGUF Q4_K在相同磁盘大小下优于NF4，是质量-内存前沿的Pareto胜者。消融实验表明FFN下投影保护是主导质量杠杆。INT8权重大小与FP8相当，速度提升需融合INT8内核。

Conclusion: INT8量化可以在缺乏FP8支持的Ampere GPU上达到FP8质量水平，但内存占用未减少，速度优势需依赖硬件内核融合。GGUF Q4_K在质量-效率权衡上表现最优。

Abstract: Post-training quantization lets large text-to-image diffusion transformers run on consumer GPUs, yet the hardware-specific trade-offs are seldom measured directly. We quantize Ideogram 4.0 - a 9.3B flow-matching diffusion transformer (DiT), shipped as two separate-weight copies of a single-stream 34-layer backbone for classifier-free guidance and conditioned by a Qwen3-VL-8B encoder - for Ampere RTX 3090 GPUs, which lack FP8 tensor cores. Our INT8 W8A8 recipe (per-channel weights, per-token dynamic activations, SmoothQuant, and mixed-precision protection of a small high-fragility layer set) holds the FP8 quality ceiling: on a 200-prompt benchmark the paired same-seed bootstrap CI for INT8-FP8 includes zero on both Pick and CLIP, while INT8 improves on NF4 by $+1.9$ CLIP (95% CI $[+1.21,+2.64]$, excluding zero). A per-category OCR analysis, to our knowledge unreported for this model class, confirms text legibility is preserved, and an ablation isolates protection of the FFN down-projections as the dominant quality lever. Our GGUF Q4_K quantization beats NF4 at equal on-disk size and is the Pareto winner on the quality-memory frontier, with paired confidence intervals excluding zero (Q8_0 is quality neutral). Finally, we characterize where 8-bit quantization helps and where it does not: INT8's weights match FP8's footprint rather than shrink it, so a speed gain on Ampere awaits a fused INT8 kernel.

</details>


### [184] [The Standard Interpretable Model: A general theory of interpretable machine learning to deductively design interpretable methods using Lagrangian mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12289)
*Pietro Barbiero,Giovanni De Felice,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Francesco Giannini,Filippo Bonchi,Mateja Jamnik,Giuseppe Marra,Ruggero Noris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出标准可解释模型（SIM），一种基于拉格朗日力学的通用理论框架，能够从用户对可解释性的前提假设出发，通过演绎推导可解释性对称性与约束，进而构造损失函数（拉格朗日量）并求解最优可解释模型。实验证明SIM能识别并解决现有方法（传统、概念、机制可解释性）的局限性，揭示未探索方向，并指导核心编程接口设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性领域缺乏通用理论来演绎设计可解释方法，导致文献碎片化且评估协议不一致。现有可解释性方法大多是启发式或经验驱动，缺少统一的理论基础。

Method: 基于拉格朗日力学的SIM框架：从用户定义的可解释性前提假设出发，推导可解释性对称性与约束，构建拉格朗日量（其最小值对应最优可解释模型），通过更新不透明模型参数或编译约束到可解释架构两种方式求解。

Result: SIM能识别并解决传统、概念、机制可解释性方法的局限性；揭示未充分探索的研究方向；指导核心编程接口设计。

Conclusion: SIM不仅是一种研究方法，其演绎性质还可为可解释性课程提供教学基础，可能改变该碎片化领域的科学共同体视角。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence models grow in complexity, interpretability has become an indispensable tool for understanding, debugging, and controlling their computations. However, interpretability lacks general theories to deductively design interpretable methods. This gap between theories and methods results in a fragmented literature and inconsistent evaluation protocols.
  To fill this gap, we introduce the Standard Interpretable Model (SIM), a general theory grounded in Lagrangian mechanics that enables the deductive design of interpretable methods. Specifically, the SIM summarises, in a set of premises, what interpretability is for a target user. From these premises, the SIM systematically derives interpretability symmetries and corresponding constraints, which shape the landscape of a Lagrangian whose minima correspond to optimal interpretable models. To reach the minima, one can either update the parameter values of an opaque model to make it more interpretable or compile constraints into an interpretable architecture.
  We empirically show that the SIM identifies and solves limitations of existing methods (including traditional, concept-based, and mechanistic interpretability), highlights underexplored research directions, and informs the design of core programming interfaces. Beyond being a research method, the deductive nature of the SIM offers pedagogical grounding for interpretability curricula and may shift the scientific community's perspective of a discipline that has long been fragmented.

</details>


### [185] [Harness In-Context Operator Learning with Chain of Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12318)
*Minghui Yang,Ling Guo,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Chain of Operators (CHOP)框架，通过构建由显式初等变换和冻结的ICON组成的算子链，使预训练ICON能够无需参数更新即可泛化到分布外算子任务，在标量守恒律和平均场控制问题上降低了推理误差，且算子链具有可解释性和跨族泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICON虽然能通过上下文提示适应不同算子，但在分布外（OOD）算子任务上泛化能力仍然不足，需要一种无需微调或参数更新的方法提升其OOD泛化性能。

Method: 受大语言模型提示工程启发，提出CHOP框架：冻结ICON参数，构建由显式初等变换（如缩放、移位等）和冻结ICON组成的算子链，通过链式组合逐步将OOD算子任务映射回模型能力范围内进行处理。

Result: 在标量守恒律和平均场控制问题上的实验表明，CHOP相比直接使用ICON评估，降低了相对推理误差；且在一个PDE族上构建的算子链能够泛化到另一个不同PDE族。

Conclusion: CHOP通过构建可解释的闭环算子链，有效提升了冻结神经算子模型的OOD泛化能力，且揭示了不同动力系统之间可能存在共享的底层机制。

Abstract: Neural operators approximate mappings between function spaces, but often generalize poorly to other operators and usually require fine-tuning or retraining. In-Context Operator Networks (ICON) addresses this issue by prompting the model with numerical context so that the model learns specific operators from prompts and adapt to different operators without fine-tuning. However, ICON may still fail to generalize to out-of-distribution (OOD) operator tasks. Inpired by the success of harness engineering of Large Language models (LLMs), we introduce Chain of Operators (CHOP), a framework that harness a frozen ICON to OOD operator tasks without updating its parameters. Specifically, CHOP constructs a chain of operators consisting of explicit elementary transformations and the frozen ICON. Experiments on a scalar conservation law and a mean-field control problem show that CHOP reduces relative inference error over direct ICON evaluation, while each operator in the chain remains interpretable and in closed form. A chain constructed on one PDE family further generalizes to a different family, indicating shared mechanisms across harness systems.

</details>


### [186] [Fourier Features Let Agents Learn High Precision Policies with Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12334)
*Balázs Gyenes,Emiliyan Gospodinov,Jan Frieling,Enrico Krohmer,Nicolas Schreiber,Xiaogang Jia,Niklas Freymuth,Gerhard Neumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对点云策略在操纵任务中性能欠佳的问题，本文提出将点云从笛卡尔空间映射到高维傅里叶空间，以缓解神经网络对低频函数的谱偏置，从而让点云编码器直接获取高频特征。实验表明，该方法在RoboCasa、ManiSkill3基准测试及真实机器人上均显著提升了多种编码器架构的性能，且对超参数鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于点云的策略性能高度依赖具体任务，作者推测其原因在于神经网络存在谱偏置（spectral bias），倾向于学习低频函数，而基于笛卡尔空间特征的架构尤其受此影响，导致难以捕捉精细的几何细节用于高精度操控。

Method: 将点云从笛卡尔坐标空间通过傅里叶特征映射（Fourier Features）变换到高维傅里叶空间，使点云编码器能够直接访问高频空间信息。该方法作为一种即插即用的输入预处理模块，可集成到多种点云编码器架构中。

Result: 在RoboCasa和ManiSkill3基准测试以及真实机器人设置上，傅里叶特征显著提升了多种编码器架构在挑战性操纵任务上的表现；该方法对不同超参数设置具有鲁棒性；相比笛卡尔特征，傅里叶特征能更有效地利用几何细节信息。

Conclusion: 傅里叶特征尽管简单，但能作为基于点云的模仿学习的通用工具，有效缓解神经网络的谱偏置问题，提升策略对几何细节的利用能力，推动高精度机器人操控的性能提升。

Abstract: High-precision robotic manipulation requires fine-grained spatial reasoning that is often difficult to achieve with RGB-only policies due to depth ambiguity and perspective scale issues. Policies that leverage 3D information directly, such as those based on point clouds, offer a stronger geometric prior over purely image-based ones, yet their performance remains highly task-dependent. We hypothesize that this discrepancy may be due to the spectral bias of neural networks towards learning low frequency functions, which especially affects architectures conditioned on slow-moving Cartesian features. We thus propose to map point clouds from Cartesian space into high-dimensional Fourier space, effectively equipping the point cloud encoder with direct access to high-frequency features. We experimentally validate the use of Fourier features on challenging manipulation tasks from the RoboCasa and ManiSkill3 benchmarks and on a real robot setup. Despite their simplicity, we find that Fourier features provide significant benefits across diverse encoder architectures and benchmarks and are robust across hyperparameters. Our results indicate that Fourier features let policies leverage geometric details more effectively than Cartesian features, showing their potential as a general-purpose tool for point cloud-based imitation learning. We provide source code and videos on our project page: https://fourier-il.github.io/fourier-il

</details>


### [187] [Claw-SWE-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating OpenClaw-style Agent Harnesses on Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12344)
*Mengyu Zheng,Kai Han,Boxun Li,Haiyang Xu,Yuchuan Tian,Wei He,Hang Zhou,Jianyuan Guo,Hailin Hu,Lin Ma,Chao Xu,Guohao Dai,Lixue Xia,Yunchao Wei,Yunhe Wang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Claw-SWE-Bench，一个多语言SWE-bench风格基准测试与适配器协议，用于公平比较不同通用代理（如OpenClaw）的编码能力。通过标准化提示、运行时预算、工作空间契约、补丁提取和评估器，发现适配器设计对性能影响巨大（从19.1%提升至73.4%），且模型选择和代理框架选择对Pass@1的影响分别为29.4和27.4个百分点。该基准包含350个实例（8种语言、43个仓库）及一个80实例的Lite子集，将代理框架和成本核算作为评估的一级维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用代理（如OpenClaw）作为自主工具用户日益普及，但其编码能力难以在SWE-bench标准下衡量，因为通用代理本身不满足SWE-bench所需的干净Docker工作空间、补丁格式和预测合约要求。因此需要一种统一的适配协议和基准，使不同代理框架在公平条件下可比。

Method: 提出Claw-SWE-Bench基准测试和适配器协议，包含：(1)固定提示、运行时预算、工作空间契约、补丁提取程序和评估器；(2)从SWE-bench-Multilingual和SWE-bench-Verified-Mini经未来提交清理后构建350个实例，覆盖8种语言43个仓库；(3)通过成本感知和排名感知的17列校准程序选出80实例的Lite子集；(4)在OpenClaw×9模型和5代理框架×2模型上进行系统评估。

Result: OpenClaw使用最小直接差异适配器仅得19.1% Pass@1，而完整适配器在相同GLM 5.1骨干模型下达到73.4%。模型选择变化导致Pass@1变化29.4个百分点，代理框架选择在固定模型下导致27.4个百分点变化。准确率相似的系统在API总成本上可能存在显著差异。

Conclusion: Claw-SWE-Bench将代理框架适配和成本核算作为SWE风格编码代理评估的一级维度，提供了完整基准和低成本参考集，支持可重复比较。

Abstract: General-purpose agents such as OpenClaw are increasingly used as autonomous tool users, but their coding ability is difficult to measure under SWE-bench: a generic agent does not by itself satisfy the clean Docker workspace, patch, and prediction contract required for scoring. We introduce Claw-SWE-Bench, a multilingual SWE-bench-style benchmark and adapter protocol that makes heterogeneous agent harnesses, or claws, comparable under fair settings including a fixed prompt, runtime budget, workspace contract, patch extraction procedure, and evaluator. The full benchmark contains 350 GitHub issue-resolution instances across 8 languages and 43 repositories, drawn from SWE-bench-Multilingual and SWE-bench-Verified-Mini after future-commit cleanup. We also release Claw-SWE-Bench Lite for faster validation, which is an 80-instance subset selected by a cost-aware, rank-aware procedure over 17 calibration columns. On the full benchmark, OpenClaw with a minimal direct-diff adapter scores only $19.1\%$ Pass@1, whereas the full adapter reaches $73.4\%$ with the same GLM 5.1 backbone, showing that adapter design is essential for enabling OpenClaw-style harnesses to perform coding tasks effectively. Across an OpenClaw $\times$ nine-model sweep and a five-claw $\times$ two-model sweep, model choice changes Pass@1 by $29.4$ pp and harness choice by $27.4$ pp under fixed models; systems with similar accuracy can differ substantially in total API cost. Claw-SWE-Bench therefore treats harness and cost accounting as first-class axes of SWE-style coding-agent evaluation, providing both a full benchmark and a low-cost reference set for reproducible comparison. The data is available at https://github.com/opensquilla/claw-swe-bench and https://huggingface.co/datasets/TokenRhythm/Claw-SWE-Bench.

</details>


### [188] [Anatomy of Post-Training: Using Interpretability to Characterize Data and Shape the Learning Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12360)
*Leon Bergen,Usha Bhalla,Sidharth Baskaran,Max Loeffler,Raphael Sarfati,Dhruvil Gala,Ryan Panwar,Santiago Aranguri,Thomas Fel,Atticus Geiger,Matthew Kowal,Siddharth Boppana,Daniel Balsam,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo,Thomas McGrath,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可解释性的数据驱动后训练流程，通过检查偏好数据集中的潜在概念来诊断和修正模型学习到的虚假相关性，从而替代传统标量奖励优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言模型后训练依赖标量奖励优化，导致实践者无法了解数据实际教会了模型什么，模型容易学习虚假相关性（如过度风格化和谄媚行为）。作者希望能在优化前检查偏好数据集，从概念层面决定模型应被允许学习哪些行为。

Method: 引入数据驱动的后训练流程：使用可解释性协议开发统计假设，识别偏好/非偏好生成之间的潜在概念；将这些概念显式化用于细粒度用户反馈；统一多种基于可解释性的训练协议，通过特征或数据干预来重塑奖励。

Result: 实验表明该流程能诊断现有偏好数据中的不良信号，缓解非目标学习，并帮助放大或塑造期望属性（如安全防护和模型个性）。

Conclusion: 可解释性可以将后训练从优化不透明的代理奖励转变为审计和塑造学习信号本身的过程，为模型行为控制提供更精细的粒度。

Abstract: Language-model post-training is the main stage at which model behavior is shaped, yet it still largely involves optimization of scalar rewards that summarize diverse desiderata. This abstraction gives practitioners little visibility into what their data actually teaches models, allowing spurious correlations to be learned by a model and inducing undesirable behaviors such as over-stylization and sycophancy. To address this problem, we ask: can we inspect a preference dataset before optimization and decide, at the level of concepts, which behaviors a model should be allowed to learn? Motivated by this, we introduce a data-centric post-training pipeline that uses interpretability protocols to develop statistical hypotheses for the latent concepts separating preferred from dispreferred generations, making them explicit for fine-grained user feedback. Building on this view, we unify several interpretability-based training protocols as ways of shaping rewards via feature or data interventions. Empirically, we show that our pipeline diagnoses undesirable signals in existing preference data, mitigates off-target learning, and can also help amplify or shape desired properties such as safeguards and model personality. More broadly, our results suggest that interpretability can turn post-training from optimizing opaque proxy rewards into a process of auditing and sculpting the learning signal itself.

</details>


### [189] [Latent World Recovery for Multimodal Learning with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12362)
*Hui Wang,Tianyu Ren,Joseph Butler,Christopher Baker,Karen Rafferty,Simon McDade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Latent World Recovery (LWR)框架，通过邻域对齐的潜在空间和可用性感知的模态融合，在模态缺失条件下实现鲁棒的多模态预测，在多组学基准上验证了癌症表型分类和生存预测的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物科学应用中异构模态往往只能部分获取，现有方法需要固定模态集或对缺失模态进行插补，导致误差传播，亟需一种能适应任意模态可用性的鲁棒多模态学习方法。

Method: LWR框架包含两个核心：(i) 将不同模态的专用嵌入对齐到共享潜在空间；(ii) 仅在训练和推理时基于实际可用的模态嵌入进行融合，构建统一表示。不进行缺失模态重建，而是将各模态视为底层潜在状态的部分感知，直接基于观测模态进行可用性感知学习。

Result: 在真实世界的不完整多组学基准上评估，LWR在癌症表型分类和生存预测等下游任务上表现有效。

Conclusion: 基于邻域的潜在对齐和可用性感知的模态融合，避免了缺失模态显式重建带来的误差传播，为部分观测下的鲁棒多模态预测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We study multimodal learning under missing modalities, with particular motivation from bioscience applications in which heterogeneous modalities are often only partially available when decisions need to be made. We propose Latent World Recovery (LWR), a framework built on two key ideas: (i) modality-specific embeddings from different modalities are aligned in a shared latent space, and (ii) a unified representation is constructed by fusing only the embeddings of the modalities that are actually available at both training and inference time. Rather than imputing missing modalities or requiring a fixed modality set, LWR treats each modality as a partial perception of an underlying latent state and performs availability-aware representation learning directly from the observed modalities. This combination of neighbor-based latent alignment and availability-aware modality fusion enables robust multimodal prediction under partial observation, while avoiding error propagation from explicit reconstruction of missing modalities. We evaluate the proposed framework on real-world incomplete multi-omics benchmarks and demonstrate that it provides an effective approach to downstream tasks such as cancer phenotype classification and survival prediction.

</details>


### [190] [On Subquadratic Architectures: From Applications to Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12364)
*Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,Levente Zólyomi,David Stap,Pieter-Jan Hoedt,Niklas Schmidinger,Lukas Hauzenberger,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet三种次二次复杂度架构，在代码模型预训练、大模型蒸馏和时间序列基础模型预训练三个复杂任务上，xLSTM取得了最优性能。通过统一框架分析，xLSTM的优势源于其门控机制实现了更灵活稳定的记忆修正和状态跟踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的二次注意力计算成本高昂，次二次复杂度架构虽可扩展，但何种设计最有效尚不明确，亟需系统性比较与分析。

Method: 在代码模型预训练、大模型蒸馏和时间序列基础模型预训练三个复杂依赖任务上，对xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet进行对比评估；提出统一公式化框架分析底层架构机制，重点关注状态跟踪和记忆动态；并通过受控合成长度泛化任务验证发现。

Result: xLSTM在所有三个复杂任务上均取得最优整体性能；其门控方案实现了更灵活稳定的记忆修正；在合成长度泛化任务上也验证了这一优势。

Conclusion: xLSTM在复杂任务上的增益源于其稳健的状态跟踪和累积能力，这为次二次复杂度序列模型的设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Transformers dominate modern sequence modeling, but their quadratic attention incurs substantial computational cost. Subquadratic architectures offer a scalable alternative. However, it remains unclear which designs yield the most effective sequence models. We compare three leading approaches: xLSTM, Mamba-2, and Gated DeltaNet. We evaluate these models on tasks with complex dependencies: (1) code-model pre-training, (2) distillation of code models from large language models, and (3) pre-training of time-series foundation models. Across these settings, xLSTM delivers the strongest overall performance. To explain xLSTM's advantage, we present a unified formulation and analyze the underlying architectural mechanisms, focusing on state tracking and memory dynamics. Our results show that xLSTM enables more flexible and stable memory correction via its gating scheme. We corroborate these findings on controlled synthetic length-generalization tasks. Overall, our findings indicate that xLSTM's gains on complex tasks stem from robust state tracking and accumulation.

</details>


### [191] [Breaking Entropy Bounds: Accelerating RL Training via MTP with Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12370)
*Yucheng Li,Huiqiang Jiang,Yang Xu,Jianxin Yang,Yi Zhang,Yizhong Cao,Yuhao Shen,Fan Zhou,Rui Men,Jianwei Zhang,An Yang,Bowen Yu,Bo Zheng,Fei Huang,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文Bebop系统研究了多token预测（MTP）在LLM后训练中的应用，发现MTP接受率受模型熵波动限制（负线性关系），提出端到端TV损失函数优化拒绝采样接受率（提升约10%），并结合预RL的MTP训练策略，在数学推理、代码生成和智能体任务上实现最高1.8x的端到端异步RL加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练中rollout阶段是主要瓶颈，MTP虽可通过推测解码加速，但RL训练中接受率显著下降导致加速有限，亟需系统研究并解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 揭示MTP接受率与模型熵的负线性关系；2) 采用概率拒绝采样替代贪婪草稿采样减轻熵干扰；3) 提出端到端TV损失直接优化多步拒绝采样接受率；4) 验证预RL的MTP训练（结合端到端TV损失+拒绝采样）即可保持稳定接受率，无需在线更新MTP。

Result: 接受率提升约10%（最高达95%），推理吞吐额外提升最高25%，在Qwen3.5/3.6/3.7模型上实现最高1.8x端到端异步RL训练加速。

Conclusion: 通过熵分析、拒绝采样优化和端到端TV损失，可以高效地将MTP集成到大规模RL流水线中，实现显著的训练加速，且预训练后无需在线更新MTP。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key component in modern large language models, yet the rollout stage remains the key bottleneck in RL training pipelines. Although Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) offers a natural solution to accelerate rollouts through speculative decoding, many studies have observed that MTP acceptance rates degrade significantly during RL training, leading to limited speedup performance. To address this bottleneck, we present Bebop, a systematic study of MTP in LLM post-training, and offer practical recipes to integrate MTP into large-scale RL pipelines. First, we reveal that the MTP acceptance rate is fundamentally bounded by the fluctuation of model entropy, which demonstrates a clear negative linear relationship with the rise of entropy in the RL stage. Second, we show that probabilistic rejection sampling largely alleviates the disturbance introduced by entropy in RL compared to greedy draft sampling. We further identify that the conventional MTP training objectives (cross-entropy or KL) are suboptimal in such settings, and therefore we propose a novel end-to-end TV loss that directly optimizes multi-step rejection sampling acceptance rate, yielding ~10% acceptance rate improvements, achieving up to 95% acceptance rates and up to 25% extra inference throughput gains across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic tasks. Third, we test various online MTP training strategies during RL and show that pre-RL MTP training with e2e TV loss and rejection sampling achieves a consistent acceptance rate and speedup throughout the entire RL, eliminating the need for costly online MTP updating. We provide extensive experiments and analysis that validate our findings. Experimental results show our method achieves up to 1.8x end-to-end acceleration in async RL training of Qwen3.5, Qwen3.6, and Qwen3.7 models.

</details>


### [192] [APPO: Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12384)
*Xucong Wang,Ziyu Ma,Yong Wang,Yuxiang Ji,Shidong Yang,Guanhua Chen,Pengkun Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出APPO方法，通过细粒度的决策点分支和信用分配来改进智能体强化学习，在13个基准测试中相比强基线的提升约4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体RL方法在粗粒度单元（如工具调用边界或固定工作流）上分配信用，难以识别哪些中间决策影响下游结果。研究表明影响力决策点广泛分布于生成序列中，而非集中在工具调用处，且仅靠token熵无法可靠反映其对最终结果的影响。

Method: 提出Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)，包含两个核心设计：(1) 使用结合token不确定性和策略诱导的后续序列似然增益的Branching Score选择分支位置，实现更精准的探索并过滤虚假高熵位置；(2) 引入过程级优势缩放（procedure-level advantage scaling）以在分支展开中更好地分配信用。

Result: 在13个基准测试上，APPO一致性地将强智能体RL基线提升近4个百分点，同时保持高效的工具调用和可解释的行为。

Conclusion: APPO通过将分支和信用分配从粗粒度交互单元转向细粒度决策点，显著改进了智能体强化学习的效果，验证了细粒度决策点识别在agentic RL中的重要性。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic Reinforcement Learning (RL) have substantially improved the multi-turn tool-use capabilities of large language model agents. However, most existing methods assign credit over coarse heuristic units, such as tool-call boundaries or fixed workflows, making it difficult to identify which intermediate decisions influence downstream outcomes. In this work, we study agentic RL from two perspectives: \textit{where to branch and how to assign credit after branching}. Our pilot analysis shows that influential decision points are broadly distributed throughout the generated sequence rather than concentrated at tool calls, while token entropy alone does not reliably reflect their impact on final outcomes. Motivated by these observations, we propose \textbf{Agentic Procedural Policy Optimization (APPO)}, which shifts branching and credit assignment from coarse interaction units to fine-grained decision points in the sequence. APPO selects branching locations using a Branching Score that combines token uncertainty with policy-induced likelihood gains of subsequent continuations, enabling more targeted exploration while filtering out spurious high-entropy positions. It further introduces procedure-level advantage scaling to better distribute credit across branched rollouts. Experiments on 13 benchmarks show that APPO consistently improves strong agentic RL baselines by nearly 4 points, while keeping efficient tool-calls and maintaining behavior interpretability.

</details>


### [193] [ATLAS: Active Theory Learning for Automated Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12386)
*Noémi Éltető,Nathaniel D. Daw,Kimberly L. Stachenfeld,Kevin J. Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ATLAS是一个主动学习框架，通过迭代生成可解释的稀疏神经网络假设并设计最优区分性实验，在bandit任务中高效恢复强化学习智能体的行为模型，相比随机实验实现5-10倍的样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学建模需要提出最优的实验问题以获取最大信息量的数据，但目前缺乏自动化这一过程的系统框架。本文旨在通过主动学习自动化认知科学中可解释行为模型的发现过程。

Method: ATLAS框架包含两个核心循环：(1) 生成机制性假设——实例化为多样化稀疏神经网络集成（Disentangled RNNs）；(2) 设计能够最优区分这些假设的实验。在bandit任务中用于恢复强化学习智能体的行为。

Result: ATLAS在所有评估指标上相比随机实验实现了5-10倍的样本效率提升，其性能通过与专家设计实验的对比得到进一步验证。

Conclusion: ATLAS在认知科学及其他依赖机制性模型发现的科学领域具有加速人类可解释洞察的潜力，展示了主动学习驱动科学发现的可行性。

Abstract: Advancing scientific understanding through mechanistic modeling requires posing the right experimental questions to yield maximally informative data. To automate this pursuit within cognitive science, we introduce ATLAS (Active Theory Learning for Automated Science), an active learning framework for the data-driven discovery of interpretable behavioral models. ATLAS iterates between generating mechanistic hypotheses--instantiated as a diverse ensemble of sparse neural networks (Disentangled RNNs)--and designing experiments that optimally distinguish between them. We test this approach on the problem of recovering reinforcement learning agents from their behavior in bandit tasks. ATLAS designs varied sequences of qualitatively novel experiments with temporal structure tailored to underlying agent characteristics. The models trained on these experiments are evaluated against a comprehensive set of metrics for mechanistic modeling that capture behavioral, structural, and computational similarity. ATLAS achieves a 5-10x improvement in sample efficiency across all metrics compared to random experimentation, and its performance is further validated against expert-designed experiments derived from literature. These in silico results showcase ATLAS's potential to accelerate human-interpretable insights in cognitive science and other domains where scientific inquiry relies on discovering mechanistic models.

</details>


### [194] [Redesign Mixture-of-Experts Routers with Manifold Power Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12397)
*Songhao Wu,Ang Lv,Ruobing Xie,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将MoE模型中的路由矩阵行与对应专家矩阵的主奇异方向对齐，并设计了基于流形幂迭代（MPI）的"幂迭代-回缩"路由重设计方法，在1B到11B参数的MoE预训练中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE路由矩阵各行本应充当专家矩阵的代理向量，通过点积反映token与专家的亲和度，但缺乏设计原则来强制实现这种"压缩"编码，导致路由未能充分表达专家矩阵的信息。

Method: 提出Manifold Power Iteration (MPI)方法，采用"Power-then-Retract"范式：先对路由权重执行幂迭代步骤使其向专家矩阵的主奇异方向收敛，再通过回缩操作施加范数约束以保证效率与稳定性。

Result: 在1B到11B参数的MoE模型预训练中，MPI方法使路由行与专家主奇异方向对齐，训练出更有效的MoE模型。

Conclusion: 将路由行与专家矩阵主奇异方向对齐是一个有效的设计原则，MPI方法能理论保证收敛并实际提升MoE模型性能，为路由设计提供了新的指导方向。

Abstract: Router is the cornerstone component to the Mixture-of-Experts models. Serving as expert proxies, the rows of the router matrix compute their similarity to the MoE inputs to determine which subset of experts is activated. Ideally, each router row is designed to encode the expert matrix into this representative vector, such that its dot-product with token can better reflect token-expert affinity. However, there exists no design principles to enforce this condensation. In this paper, we propose to align each router row with the principal singular direction of the associated expert, as this direction provides the most expressive mathematical description of a matrix. Based on this principle, we propose a router redesign with Manifold Power Iteration (MPI). Specifically, it introduces a "Power-then-Retract" paradigm, where a power iteration step is performed on the router weights, followed by a retraction to impose a norm constraint to ensure both efficiency and stability. Theoretically, we show that MPI drives router rows to converge toward the principal singular directions of associated experts. Empirically, we pretrain MoE model across scales from 1B to 11B parameters to confirm that this alignment facilitates more effective MoE models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [From Explicit Elements to Implicit Intent: A Predefined Library for Auditable Behavioral Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11207)
*Liu hung ming*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemantiClean是一个模块化框架，从电商会话数据中提取结构化语义信号，驱动购买意图、客户细分和产品关联等推理目标。它基于OSPI数据集，将24个行为元素组织为四层架构（功能、交互、系统、上下文），并通过三种反通胀机制保证信号质量。报告引入了LLM集成语义推理引擎，实现两阶段LLM驱动的推理架构，其结果分为确定性可复现部分和LLM依赖的可控变异部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端预测模型仅优化准确率，缺乏可审计性、结构治理和可复现性，难以提供元素级别的透明度和可辩护的决策轨迹。

Method: 设计四层架构（Functional, Interaction, Systemic, Contextual）组织24个行为元素，引入三种反通胀机制（RedundancyGroup贡献上限、TieredPenaltyCalculator偏差惩罚、AdaptiveConstraintMode冷启动保护），并构建两阶段LLM驱动的推理引擎。

Result: 确定性引擎输出完全可复现（sigma=0）；LLM依赖结果（E8, E10）在固定提供者/模型/温度设置下具有可控输出变异性；性别推理目标当前未实现，已排除在定量结果之外。

Conclusion: SemantiClean通过牺牲边际预测增益，换取了元素级透明度和可辩护的决策轨迹，实现了可审计、可治理、可复现的结构化语义信号提取框架。

Abstract: We present SemantiClean, a modular framework for extracting structured semantic signals from e-commerce session data and driving pluggable inference targets including purchase intent, customer segmentation, and product affinity through a shared element library. Unlike conventional end-to-end predictors that optimise solely for accuracy, SemantiClean prioritises auditability, structural governance, and sigma=0 reproducibility, explicitly trading marginal predictive gains for element-level transparency and defensible decision trails. Built upon the Online Shoppers Purchasing Intention (OSPI) dataset, the framework organises twenty-four behavioural elements into a four-layer architecture (Functional, Interaction, Systemic, Contextual) and enforces signal quality through three anti-inflation mechanisms: RedundancyGroup contribution caps, TieredPenaltyCalculator bias penalties, and AdaptiveConstraintMode cold-start protection.This report introduces the LLM-Integrated Semantic Inference Engine, a fully implemented two-phase LLM-driven inference architecture that leverages complete element metadata at inference time. All quantitative results reported herein are produced by this engine. Deterministic engine outputs remain fully reproducible (sigma=0); LLM-dependent results (E8, E10) are subject to controlled output variability under fixed provider/model/temperature settings. The gender inference target remains non-functional in the current implementation and is excluded from all quantitative results.

</details>


### [196] [Position: Hippocampal Explicit Memory Is the Cornerstone for AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11245)
*Sangjun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将显式记忆集成到大语言模型中是推动其迈向通用人工智能的关键，因为LLM的学习机制类似于人类内隐记忆，而AGI所需的高级认知功能依赖于海马体的外显记忆系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM虽表现出色，但其学习机制仅相当于人类内隐记忆，无法支撑AGI所需的高级认知功能（如长期战略规划、元认知、符号推理），因此需要引入显式记忆系统来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 从神经科学发现出发，论证LLM学习机制与人类内隐记忆的相似性，并指出AGI所需能力与海马体外显记忆的依赖关系，进而提出人工显式记忆系统的计算需求。

Result: 明确了LLM与人类内隐记忆的类比关系，指出了显式记忆对于AGI的必要性，并提出了构建人工显式记忆系统的计算要求。

Conclusion: 集成显式记忆是推动LLM迈向AGI的基石，这一方向值得进一步研究，并为显式记忆集成奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across various tasks, raising expectations for Artificial General Intelligence (AGI). This position paper argues that integrating explicit memory is the cornerstone for advancing LLMs toward AGI. The key reason is that the underlying learning mechanism of LLMs is highly analogous to human implicit memory. However, higher-order cognitive functions necessary for AGI, such as long-term strategic planning, metacognition, and symbolic reasoning, heavily rely on hippocampal explicit memory and cannot arise solely from implicit statistical learning. Drawing on findings from neuroscience, I advance this perspective and complement it with computational requirements for artificial explicit memory systems, hoping to foster further research and lay the groundwork for explicit memory integration.

</details>


### [197] [Can AI Agents Synthesize Scientific Conclusions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11337)
*Hayoung Jung,Pedro Viana Diniz,José Reinaldo Corrêa Roveda,Abner Fernandes da Silva,Haeun Jung,Enoch Tsai,Aleksandra Korolova,Manoel Horta Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciConBench——一个包含9.11K问题的大规模实时基准，用于评估科学结论合成能力，并配套推出SciConHarness洁净室评估框架以防止数据泄露。对8个前沿模型和深度研究代理的评估显示，最佳代理在洁净室设置下的事实F1仅为0.337；消费级代理（如Google AI Overview、OpenEvidence）在已有参考答案的情况下仍频繁生成不完整甚至矛盾的结论。研究揭示可靠的科学结论合成仍是开放难题，洁净室评估对衡量开放域AI代理的真实能力至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学AI代理越来越多地用于检索证据、跨来源推理并综合结论，这些结论被用于高风险决策（如健康领域）。然而，这类代理在高风险领域（尤其是医疗健康）中进行结论合成的实际能力尚不明确，现有评估方法存在数据泄露风险，导致高估模型真实能力。

Method: 1) 构建SciConBench基准，包含9.11K问题和来自系统综述的专家撰写结论；2) 设计专家验证的自动化评估管道，将结论分解为原子事实，通过事实精确率和召回率衡量正确性和全面性；3) 引入SciConHarness洁净室评估框架，通过可控的Web交互防止数据泄露；4) 评估8个前沿模型和深度研究代理，并审计消费级代理（Google AI Overview、OpenEvidence）。

Result: 洁净室设置下最佳代理的事实F1仅为0.337；洁净室设置始终降低模型性能，表明数据泄露会高估模型真实合成能力；消费级代理频繁生成不完整甚至矛盾的结论，即便已有参考答案。

Conclusion: 可靠的科学结论合成仍是开放挑战；洁净室评估对于准确评估开放域AI代理的真实能力至关重要；现有模型和代理在科学结论合成方面性能不足，亟需改进。

Abstract: Scientific AI agents increasingly retrieve evidence, reason across sources, and synthesize conclusions used in consequential decisions. Yet, their ability to do so in high-stakes domains such as health remains unclear. We introduce SciConBench, a large-scale live benchmark of 9.11K questions and expert-written conclusions from systematic reviews to evaluate open-domain scientific conclusion synthesis. The benchmark draws on an expert-validated automated evaluation pipeline that decomposes conclusions into atomic facts and measures correctness and comprehensiveness via factual precision and recall. To mitigate data leakage, we further introduce SciConHarness, a clean-room evaluation harness that equips agents with controlled web interaction to ensure valid measurement. Evaluating 8 frontier models and deep research agents, we find that factual quality remains low: under clean-room settings, the best agent achieves only a factual F1 of 0.337. Our clean-room setting consistently reduces performance relative to unconstrained evaluation, suggesting that leakage inflates estimates of models' true synthesis capabilities. Finally, we audit consumer-facing agents (e.g., Google AI Overview, OpenEvidence) and find they frequently generate incomplete and sometimes contradictory conclusions, even when the ground-truth answer is available. Overall, our results show that reliable synthesis of scientific conclusions remains an open challenge, and that clean-room evaluation is essential for assessing open-domain AI agents.

</details>


### [198] [Knowing When to Ask: Self-Gated Clarification for Hierarchical Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11349)
*Aijing Gao,Yiming Kang,Mengdie Flora Wang,Jae Oh Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ACTION-RATING，将信息寻求内置于agent的动作空间，与导航动作共享序数量表，使询问与行动在每个决策点直接竞争。在30,000节点的HS分类任务中，观察到从强制性澄清到机会性澄清的转变，信息寻求有效性（ISE）从50%提升至74%，在受控答案通道下10-digit准确率提升+16.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次推理中，失败常源于中间决策点agent提交错误分支而未意识到缺少关键信息。现有方法将澄清视为外部不确定性触发，未能将其与导航动作在同一尺度上竞争。

Method: 提出ACTION-RATING框架，将信息寻求置于agent动作空间内部，与导航共享序数量表。定义两种信息寻求模式：强制性（无可行分支）和机会性（领先候选项仍存在残差不确定性）。引入局部诊断指标ISE（信息寻求有效性）。

Result: 在30,000节点的HS分类任务、三个基准、4个家族9个LLM上，ISE从50%提升至74%；三种诊断对比未能复现该结构；可分离性测试表明信息寻求模式在答案质量下降-18.8%时仍保持；受控答案通道下10-digit准确率增益+16.2%。

Conclusion: 将该增益解读为更好定位所能解锁的上界，而非部署估计。信息寻求模式（模式划分、ISE排序）与所获帮助质量之间存在经验性分离。

Abstract: In hierarchical reasoning, failures often originate at intermediate decision points where the agent commits to a wrong branch without recognizing that it lacks critical information. Rather than treating clarification as an external uncertainty trigger, we propose ACTION-RATING, a formulation that places it inside the agent's action space on a shared ordinal scale with navigation, so that asking competes directly with acting at every decision point and help-seeking becomes observable at intermediate states. Two structurally distinct information-seeking modes emerge from the agent's own ratings: mandatory (no viable branch) and opportunistic (residual uncertainty despite a leading candidate). On Harmonized Tariff Schedule classification (30,000-node taxonomy, three benchmarks, 9~LLMs across 4 families), we observe a regime shift from mandatory to opportunistic clarification, with Information-Seeking Effectiveness (ISE), a local diagnostic defined as the fraction of help interactions followed by a correct next navigation step (not a final-task metric), rising from 50% to 74%. Three diagnostic contrasts fail to reproduce this structure. A separability test shows that the information-seeking pattern (mode split, ISE ranking) persists when answer quality is degraded (-18.8% accuracy), supporting an empirical separation between where an agent seeks help and the quality of the help it receives. Under the controlled answer channel, accuracy gains reach +16.2% at 10-digit; we read this as an upper bound on what better localization could unlock, not a deployment estimate.

</details>


### [199] [Automated Mediator for Human Negotiation: Pre-Mediation via a Structured LLM Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11379)
*Jamie Bergen,Sarit Kraus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM模块的结构化流水线自动调解员，用于支持人类谈判前的准备阶段（预调解）。通过将预调解分解为对话、偏好预测、响应级批评和结构化总结等专门模块，该系统在两项受控人类实验中被证明能达到与专业人类调解员相当的短期自我报告准备效果，同时在偏好推理任务上误差降低36%，并通过提示优化将过度肯定模式从36.6%降至16.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预调解是达成互利协议的关键阶段，但由于成本、时间和缺乏训练有素的调解员而常被省略。现有单次提示的LLM方法存在局限性，需要更结构化的自动化方案来提供可扩展、低成本的预调解支持。

Method: 设计了一个结构化LLM流水线，包含对话模块、偏好预测模块、响应级批评模块和结构化总结模块。各模块按固定顺序前向传递输出，模块间非自主、非对等交互。在两项受控人类实验中，与专业人类调解员进行比较评估。

Result: 自动化调解员在短期自我报告指标上与人类调解员相当（包括对调解员的信任和达成互利协议的信心）；在偏好推理任务上RMSE降低36%；通过提示优化将过度肯定模式从36.6%降至16.8%，达到人类基线水平。

Conclusion: 结构化LLM流水线能够提供可扩展、低成本的预调解支持，在短期自我报告准备效果上与人类调解员相当。其单方设计镜像了人类调解员的实际工作方式，支持跨各方并行部署，具有良好的扩展性。

Abstract: Pre-mediation, the preparatory phase preceding direct human negotiation, plays a critical role in achieving mutually beneficial agreements, yet is often omitted due to cost, time, and limited access to trained mediators. We introduce an automated mediator for human negotiation, implemented as a structured pipeline of LLM modules, that supports pre-mediation in integrative negotiation settings. The pipeline decomposes preparation into specialized modules for dialogue, preference prediction, response-level critique, and structured summarization, separating inference, generation, and evaluation to address limitations of monolithic single-prompt approaches. We use the term "agent" for each module following common LLM-systems terminology, but the components are not autonomous and do not interact peer-to-peer; outputs are passed forward in a fixed sequence. We evaluate the system in two controlled human-subject experiments comparing AI-based pre-mediation with professional human mediators in a multi-issue negotiation scenario. On short-term self-reported measures, the automated mediator achieves preparation outcomes broadly comparable to human mediators, including trust in the mediator and confidence in reaching mutually beneficial agreements, while achieving substantially lower error on the preference-inference task under our scenario and prompts (36% lower RMSE). A second study shows that targeted prompt refinements reduce excessive affirmation patterns from 36.6% to 16.8%, matching human mediator baselines. Our findings suggest that structured LLM pipelines can provide scalable, low-effort pre-mediation support broadly comparable to human mediators on short-term self-reported preparation outcomes. The pipeline's single-party design mirrors how human mediators run pre-mediation today and enables parallel deployment across all parties to a dispute, supporting scalability.

</details>


### [200] [INFRAMIND: Infrastructure-Aware Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11440)
*Ahasan Kabir,Jiaqi Xue,Mengxin Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: INFRAMIND是一个面向多智能体LLM编排的基础设施感知框架，通过强化学习将运行时基础设施状态（队列深度、KV缓存压力、延迟等）纳入规划、路由和调度决策，在共享GPU集群下实现了高达+7.6%的准确率提升、7倍延迟降低以及99.9%的SLO合规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM编排方法（暴力集成、学习路由器等）仅基于任务和模型特征进行选择，忽略了共享GPU集群上服务基础设施的运行时状态，导致资源利用不均：首选模型请求队列堆积，而同等能力的替代模型闲置。在多智能体流水线中，这种延迟会向下游逐级放大。

Method: 提出INFRAMIND框架，包含三个核心组件：1）基础设施感知的规划器，根据实时系统负载和剩余预算选择拓扑和角色；2）基础设施感知的执行器，观测每个模型的队列深度、缓存利用率和响应延迟，决定调用哪个模型及推理深度；3）预算感知的调度器，重排各模型请求队列，优先处理紧急请求。整体建模为层次化约束MDP，通过端到端强化学习求解。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，低负载下相比基线准确率提升最高+7.6个百分点，延迟降低最高7倍；高负载下SLO合规率维持99.9%，而所有基线均降至50%以下。

Conclusion: 将基础设施运行时状态显式纳入多智能体编排的决策循环，能够大幅提升系统在动态共享GPU集群上的资源利用效率、响应延迟和服务质量保障能力。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent LLM orchestration methods, ranging from brute-force ensembles to learned routers, select models and topologies based on task and model features. However, these methods do not consider the runtime state of the serving infrastructure. On shared GPU clusters under concurrent load, this infrastructure blindness causes systematic resource underutilization: preferred models accumulate deep request queues while equally capable alternatives sit idle. In multi-agent pipelines, where each query triggers multiple sequential model calls, these delays then compound across every downstream step. Closing this gap is challenging because the relevant infrastructure signals (queue depths, KV-cache pressure, latencies) are dynamic and noisy, and they must drive three different decisions: planning, per-step routing, and scheduling. We introduce INFRAMIND, a framework that makes the entire multi-agent stack infrastructure-aware. An infra-aware planner conditions topology and role selection on real-time system load and remaining budget, biasing toward simpler graphs under congestion and richer ones at low load. An infra-aware executor then observes per-model queue depths, cache utilization, and response latencies at each agent step to decide which model to call and how deeply to reason; a budget-aware scheduler further reorders each model's queue so that urgent requests are served first. Cast as a hierarchical constrained MDP and solved end-to-end via reinforcement learning, the system learns to balance quality against latency automatically. Across five benchmarks, INFRAMIND delivers up to +7.6 pp accuracy over the prior baseline at low load with up to 7x lower latency, and sustains up to 99.9% SLO compliance under high load where every baseline drops below 50%.

</details>


### [201] [Forecasting Future Behavior as a Learning Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11445)
*Mosh Levy,Yoav Goldberg,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种替代传统解释方法的行为预测方案：训练行为预测器（Behavior Forecaster），直接基于大型推理模型（LRM）的推理轨迹预测其未来行为，无需人工标注，单次前向传播即可完成推理。在两项任务（答案重复概率、输入删减影响）和三个数据集上，该方法优于GPT-5.4和Claude Opus-4.6的朴素阅读，且推理成本极低。研究表明，推理轨迹包含超越朴素阅读所能获取的关于LRM未来行为的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于解释的AI信任建立方法在大型推理模型（LRM）上面临困难：单token生成解释方法难以推广到长轨迹，且推理轨迹本身作为自然语言阅读时往往不够忠实。因此需要一种绕过解释步骤、直接预测LRM未来行为的替代方案。

Method: 将行为预测视为可学习的任务，训练行为预测器（Behavior Forecaster）。预测器以单条推理轨迹为输入，在单次前向传播中完成预测。训练数据通过查询LRM自动生成，无需人工标注。方法包含两个关键要素：对骨干模型进行端到端微调，以及从目标LRM初始化模型参数。

Result: 在答案重复概率预测和输入删减影响预测两项任务上，基于三个不同推理数据集评估，训练得到的行为预测器在准确性上超越GPT-5.4和Claude Opus-4.6的朴素阅读，且推理成本远低于后两者。端到端微调和从目标LRM初始化参数是取得优异性能的必要条件。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹中蕴含关于LRM未来行为的信息，其丰富程度超出朴素自然语言阅读所能提取的范围。通过学习而非解释的方式利用这些信息，可以更高效、更准确地预测LRM行为，为建立对AI系统的信任提供了新途径。

Abstract: Trust in an AI system is often anchored by explanations of how it works, which one then uses to forecast its behavior on new inputs. For large reasoning models (LRMs), this conventional route is particularly difficult to follow: explanation methods for single token generations do not naturally generalize to long trajectories, and the trajectories themselves are often not faithful when read as natural language. We propose an alternative that bypasses the explanation step: treat behavior forecasting as a learnable task and train Behavior Forecasters that operates on a single reasoning trajectory to make the same forecasts one would typically seek from an explanation. The forecaster's training data is obtained by querying the LRM with no human annotation, and its inference is done in a single forward pass. We instantiate this approach on two tasks: how likely the LRM is to repeat its answer on re-runs, and how removing parts of the input changes its answer. We evaluate this approach on both tasks across three diverse reasoning datasets and find that trained Behavior Forecasters are more accurate than GPT-5.4 and Claude Opus-4.6 reading the same trajectories as naive readers, at a small fraction of their inference cost. We find that fine-tuning the backbone end-to-end and initializing it from the target LRM are each necessary for strong performance. These results show that the reasoning trajectory carries information about the LRM's future behavior that goes beyond what naive reading conveys.

</details>


### [202] [Search Discipline for Long-Horizon Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11522)
*Adithya Srinivasan,Devesh Paragiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现，当科学有效性存在于异构子群结构中时，基于聚合指标（如全局分数）进行候选选择会优先选出破坏底层结构的错误候选，导致“指标改善但结构崩溃”的反转现象。作者提出一种外部控制回路审计协议，基于可审查的候选效应证据而非单一分数做出决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动研究代理使用聚合度量来评估和选择科学候选方案，但聚合指标可能掩盖底层子群结构中的关键失效模式，导致选出的候选方案在全局分数上看似最优，实则破坏了局部区域（如北方森林）的科学有效性。作者旨在揭示这一反转现象并提出解决方案。

Method: 1) 在生态系统人口模型（EDM）的火模型任务上展示反转现象：全局分数相近的两个候选方案，高分者破坏北方森林区域而低分者保护之。2) 提出搜索纪律协议（search-discipline protocol），将决策权从代理转移到外部控制回路，该回路审计每个候选方案在去聚合结构（各区域/子群）上的行为，可在代理停止后降级其接受的候选或重新打开其已关闭的运行。

Result: 全局分数最高的候选方案与次优方案在噪声范围内，但高分候选导致北方森林区域崩溃，而次优方案保护这些区域。说明聚合指标无法区分真正的科学有效性，区分关键在于每个区域的行为，而非标题数字。

Conclusion: 当候选方案的有效性是多维的而验证器是单一降维时，反转现象普遍存在，不限于特定领域。应避免让产生候选方案的代理同时负责决策，而应采用外部审计机制，基于可审查的区域级效应证据而非聚合分数来做出最终选择。

Abstract: Autoresearch agents now propose, evaluate, and select scientific candidates against a metric, and that metric is usually an aggregate reduced over a heterogeneous space of regions, slices, or cohorts. We show that when scientific validity lives in that disaggregated structure, the aggregate can rank the wrong candidate first. The headline number improves while the structure underneath inverts, so a decision made on the number accepts a candidate that quietly breaks the model. The failure is not domain-specific. It appears wherever a candidate's validity is multi-dimensional but its verifier is a single reduction.
  We demonstrate the inversion on a fire-model task in the Ecosystem Demography model. The highest-scoring candidate and a slightly lower one are within noise of each other on global score, yet the top-scoring one collapses the protected boreal regions while the other preserves them. What separates them is the per-region behavior, not the headline number.
  This decision should not be left to the agent that produced the candidates. The agent optimizing the score is the last party likely to catch the score being wrong, and a prompt has no remaining turn once the agent has stopped. We move the decision to an external control loop that audits each candidate on its disaggregated behavior and acts after the agent has decided. It can demote a candidate the agent would have accepted, and it can reopen a run the agent had declared finished. Our contribution is the inversion finding itself, and a search-discipline protocol that decides on reviewable candidate-effect evidence instead of the score.

</details>


### [203] [MoCA-Agent: A Market-of-Claims Code Agent for Financial and Numerical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11537)
*Abdelrahman Abdallah,AbdelRahim A. Elmadany,Sameh Al Natour,Hasan Cavusoglu,Adam Jatowt,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOCA-Agent 是一种基于“观点市场”的代码智能体，将金融和表格问答任务分解为原子化声明，通过交易者智能体买卖声明、市场出清形成置信加权决策，并生成可执行Python程序，在十个公开基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融和表格问答不仅需要流畅的推理能力，答案还必须严格基于事实、公式、单位、符号和尺度。单个单元格的误读或错误操作会产生看似合理但实际错误的结果，现有自由形式的多智能体辩论方法难以保证细粒度的正确性。

Method: 将每个问题分解为类型化的原子声明，由专业交易者智能体买卖这些声明，通过市场出清机制生成置信加权的接受/拒绝决策，然后从市场支持的证据中合成可执行的Python程序，最后由代码感知验证器检查执行、结构一致性和常见金融推理错误，最多进行一轮市场感知的修复。

Result: 在十个公开基准上使用固定Qwen3.6-27B骨干网络取得强性能：FinQA 78.3%、FinanceMath 76.0%、MultiHiertt 71.2%、ESGenius 86.9%、FinChart-Bench平均85.6%。

Conclusion: 在原子化声明层面聚合证据，而非整个答案层面，能够提高高风险数值推理任务的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Financial and tabular question answering requires more than fluent reasoning: answers must be grounded in the exact facts, formulas, units, signs, and scales that support them. A single misread cell or incorrect operation can silently produce a plausible but wrong result. We introduce \textsc{MOCA-Agent}, a market-of-claims code agent that replaces free-form multi-agent debate with claim-level verification. The system decomposes each question into typed atomic claims, asks specialist trader agents to buy or sell those claims, clears their orders into confidence-weighted accept/reject decisions, and synthesizes an executable Python program from market-supported evidence. A code-aware verifier then checks the program for execution, structural consistency, and common financial reasoning errors, with at most one market-aware repair round. Across ten public benchmarks spanning financial numerical reasoning, general tabular reasoning, ESG question answering, and multimodal chart reasoning, \textsc{MOCA-Agent} achieves strong performance using a fixed Qwen3.6-27B backbone, including $78.3\%$ on FinQA, $76.0\%$ on FinanceMath, $71.2\%$ on MultiHiertt, $86.9\%$ on ESGenius, and $85.6\%$ average on FinChart-Bench. These results show that aggregating evidence at the level of atomic claims, rather than whole answers, improves robustness in high-stakes numerical reasoning.\footnote{The code and data are available: https://github.com/UBC-NLP/MoCA-Agent.

</details>


### [204] [SkillJuror: Measuring How Agent Skill Organization Changes Runtime Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11543)
*Zhiyu Chen,Zihan Guo,Bo Huang,Bingwei Lu,Jianghao Lin,Yuanjian Zhou,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillJuror框架，研究技能组织方式（渐进式披露 vs. 扁平基线）对LLM智能体行为的影响。在82个任务的SkillsBench研究中，渐进式披露使智能体调用的技能资源从1.18增至3.85，有效采纳事件从1.33增至3.92，并在410组匹配试验中多出17个通过验证的试验（+4.1%）。收益因任务而异，当资源支持实现、检查或修复时帮助大，当依赖精确输出格式、数值阈值或长流程时效果弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试很少区分技能的内容与组织方式，本文旨在探究技能的组织结构如何影响智能体的推理时行为与任务表现。

Method: 提出SkillJuror框架，通过语义控制变体、匹配多轮评估和轨迹证据，在固定任务知识前提下比较渐进式披露（简洁根文件指向支持资源）与扁平化基线两种技能编写范式。

Result: 在82个任务的SkillsBench上，渐进式披露使每条轨迹触达的不同技能资源从1.18升至3.85，有效采纳事件从1.33升至3.92；在410组匹配试验中多出17个通过验证的试验（+4.1%）。收益任务依赖：有助于实现/检查/修复类任务，弱于精确格式/数值阈值/长流水线类任务。

Conclusion: 技能组织不仅仅是呈现方式问题，它能够改变智能体搜索和应用程序性知识的行为方式，而结果收益取决于暴露的资源是否对任务可操作。

Abstract: Agent Skills augment large language model (LLM) agents with procedural knowledge at inference time, but current benchmarks rarely distinguish what a Skill says from how it is organized. We study this distinction through Progressive Disclosure, where a concise root file points agents to supporting resources on demand, and compare it with a normalized flat baseline. We present SkillJuror, a framework for evaluating Skill writing paradigms through semantically controlled variants, matched multi-trial evaluations, and trajectory evidence while holding task knowledge fixed. In an 82-task SkillsBench study, Progressive Disclosure changes runtime behavior before aggregate outcomes: distinct Skill resources touched per trajectory rise from 1.18 to 3.85, and effective uptake events rise from 1.33 to 3.92. It also yields 17 additional verifier-passing trials out of 410 matched trials (+4.1%) over the normalized flat baseline. The benefit is task-dependent. Progressive Disclosure helps when supporting resources guide implementation, checking, or repair, but is weaker when success hinges on exact output conventions, numerical thresholds, or long artifact-generation pipelines. These results show that Skill organization is not mere presentation: it can change how agents search and apply procedural knowledge, while outcome gains depend on whether the exposed resources are actionable for the task. Code is available at https://github.com/zhiyuchen-ai/skill-juror.

</details>


### [205] [HERO: Hindsight-Enhanced Reflection from Environment Observations for Agentic Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11559)
*Haoran Liu,Yuwei Zhang,Xiyao Li,Bohan Lyu,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HERO提出了一种事后增强的自蒸馏框架，通过将下一时刻的环境观察转化为紧凑的回合级诊断反馈，解决了多轮强化学习中信用分配和反馈对齐问题，在TauBench和WebShop上显著提升了任务成功率并减少了无效回合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线自蒸馏方法在多轮设定中表现不佳，因为特权反馈（如成功轨迹或终端结果）与学生当前的决策上下文之间缺乏对齐，导致信用分配困难。
